<script>
  function loadAllLazy(){
    $(window).on('load', function(){
      var $elems = $('.lazy').lazy({
        visibleOnly: false,
        onFinishedAll: function(){$elems.removeClass('lazy').addClass('lazyLoaded');}
      });
    });
  }
</script>

<!-- Animation Cart -->
<script>
  class ShoppingCartAnimation
  {
    constructor(cartSelector, maxDim, minimizeToMaxDim, scrollTime, callback)
    {
      if(typeof maxDim == 'undefined') maxDim = 250;
      if(typeof minimizeToMaxDim == 'undefined') minimizeToMaxDim = 50;
      if(typeof cartSelector == 'undefined') return console.error('ShoppingCartAnimation: cartSelector is not set in constructor.');

      this.cartSelector = cartSelector;
      this.targetMaxDim = maxDim;
      this.targetMinimizeToMaxDim = minimizeToMaxDim;
      this.scrollTime = scrollTime;
      this.callback = typeof callback == 'undefined'? null : callback;
    }

    animate($img)
    {
      var $cloneImage = this.cloneImageInFixedPosition($img);
      var thisEnt = this;

      $cloneImage.animate({opacity: '1'}, {
          duration: 300,
          complete: function(){
              thisEnt.moveCloneTowardsCart($cloneImage);
              thisEnt.scrollToTop(function(){
                thisEnt.sendCloneToCart($cloneImage, $img);
              });
          }
      })
    }

    cloneImageInFixedPosition($img)
    {
      var source = $img.attr('src'),
        prevWidth = $img.width(),
        prevHeight = $img.height(),
        maxDim = prevWidth > prevHeight? prevWidth : prevHeight,
        scaleRatio = this.targetMaxDim / maxDim,
        w = Math.floor(scaleRatio*prevWidth),
        h = Math.floor(scaleRatio*prevHeight),
        offsetW = (prevWidth - w)/2,
        offsetH = (prevHeight - h)/2,

        fixTop = $img.offset().top - $(window).scrollTop(),
        fixLeft = $img.offset().left - $(window).scrollLeft(),

        sumTop = Math.floor(fixTop + offsetH),
        sumLeft = Math.floor(fixLeft + offsetW);

        sumTop = sumTop<0? 0 : sumTop;
        sumLeft = sumLeft<0? 0 : sumLeft;

            /*var $cloneShadow = $('<div class="cloneShadow"/>').css({
          borderRadius: '50%',
          width: '100%',
          height: '100%',
          background: 'rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)'
      }); */

        return $('<div id="clone">').css({
          zIndex: 10000,
          opacity: '0',
          borderRadius: '50%',
          backgroundImage: 'url('+source+')',
          backgroundRepeat: 'no-repeat',
          backgroundPosition: 'center',
          backgroundSize: 'contain',
          width: w+"px",
          height: h+"px",
          position: "fixed",
          top: sumTop+"px",
          left: sumLeft+"px"
        })./*append($cloneShadow).*/appendTo('body');

        /*
      return $('<img id="clone" src="'+source+'" style="z-index:3; border-radius: 50%;">').css({
          width: w+"px",
          height: h+"px",
          position: "fixed",
          top: sumTop+"px",
          left: sumLeft+"px"
        }).appendTo('body');
        */
    }

    scrollToTop(callback)
    {
      if(typeof callback != 'undefined')
        $('html, body').animate({scrollTop: 0}, this.scrollTime, callback);
      else
        $('html, body').animate({scrollTop: 0}, this.scrollTime);
    }

    moveCloneTowardsCart($clone)
    {
      var $cart = $(this.cartSelector),
        targetOffset = $cart.offset();

      var thisEnt = this;
      $clone.animate({top: targetOffset.top, left: targetOffset.left},
      {
        duration: thisEnt.scrollTime
        //easing: 'swing',
      });
    }

    sendCloneToCart($clone, $img)
    {
      var $cart = $(this.cartSelector),
        targetPosition = $cart.offset();

      var prevWidth = $img.width(),
        prevHeight = $img.height(),
        maxDim = prevWidth > prevHeight? prevWidth : prevHeight,
        scaleRatio = this.targetMinimizeToMaxDim / maxDim;

      var thisEnt = this;
      $clone.animate({
        top: targetPosition.top,
        left: targetPosition.left,
        width: Math.floor(prevWidth * scaleRatio),
        height: Math.floor(prevHeight * scaleRatio)
      }, {
        duration: 150,
        complete: function(){
          $clone.remove();

          if(thisEnt.callback !== null)
            thisEnt.callback();
        }
      });
    }
  }

  function previewCart()
  {
    $('.basket .dropdown-menu').first().stop(true, true).delay(250).slideDown();
    setTimeout(function(){
        $('.basket:not(:hover) .dropdown-menu:not(:hover)').first().stop(true, true).delay(250).slideUp();
      }, 2800
    );
  }

  var shoppingCartAnimation = new ShoppingCartAnimation('.basket.dropdown', 60, 3, 800, previewCart);
</script>
<script>
    function quickViewBuy(event, caller)
    {
        var $caller = $(caller),
            $attributes = $caller.closest('.row').parent().find('.product_select'),
            productId = $caller.closest('form').find('[name="products_id"]').val(),
            quantity = $caller.closest('[class^="modal"]').find('[name="productQty"]').val();

        var attributes = [];
        $attributes.find('.row').each(function(){
            var selectTag = $(this).find('select')[0],
                attrId = selectTag.id,//ex.: id[5] //.replace('id[', '').replace(']', ''),
                optionId = selectTag.value;
            attributes.push([attrId, optionId]);
        });
        ajax_buy_now_call_with_attributes(event, productId, attributes, quantity, function(responseText){
            //No page redirect => Refresh the page.
            var documentDivParent = document.createElement("DIV");
                documentDivParent.innerHTML =  responseText;

            document.getElementsByClassName("basket")[0].innerHTML =
                documentDivParent.getElementsByClassName("basket")[0].innerHTML;

            $(".addtocartloader."+productId).removeClass("view");
            var milis = 400;
            $caller.closest('.modalreadmore.modal').fadeOut(milis);
            $('.modal-backdrop').fadeOut(
                milis,
                function(){
                    $caller.closest('.modal-content').find('.close.custom-close-1').click();

                    var $img = $caller.closest('form').find('.products-box .products-image img');
                    shoppingCartAnimation.animate($img);
                }
            );
        });
    }
    function updateQuantity(targetElement)
    {
        if(typeof targetElement == 'undefined') targetElement = $('[name="productQty"]');
        else targetElement = $(targetElement);


        var attributes = [];
        //product listing
        var $attributes = targetElement.closest('.product_theme_price').parent().find('.product_select');
        if($attributes.length == 1)
        {
            $attributes.find('select').each(function(){
                var selectTag = this,
                    attrId = selectTag.id.replace('id', 'attributes'),//ex.: id[5] //.replace('id[', '').replace(']', ''),
                    optionId = selectTag.value;
                attributes.push([attrId, optionId]);
            });
        }
        else //NOT product listing => Product info page
        {
            $attributes = targetElement.closest('form').parent().find('.select-boxes>select');
            $attributes.each(function(){
                var attrId = this.name.replace('id', 'attributes'),//ex.: id[5] //.replace('id[', '').replace(']', ''),
                    optionId = this.value;
                attributes.push([attrId, optionId]);
            });
        }

        var attributesString = "";
        attributes.forEach(function(attribute){
            attributesString += '&'+attribute[0]+'='+attribute[1];
        });

        $.get(location.href.split("?")[0]+'?action=getQuantity&products_id='+targetElement.attr('prod')+attributesString, function(max){
            var $qtyInput = targetElement;
            var intVal;
            try{intVal = parseInt($qtyInput.val()); max = parseInt(max);}
            catch(ex){$qtyInput.val(1); return;}
            if(intVal > max)
                $qtyInput.val(max);
        });
    }

    $(document).ready(function(){
        //If ENTER is hit while editting the field, IGNORE it.
        $('[name="productQty"]').keydown(function(event){if(event.keyCode == 13) {event.preventDefault(); return false;}});
        $('[name="productQty"]').change(function(){
            var $this = $(this), intVal;
            try{intVal = parseInt($this.val());}
            catch(ex){$this.val(1); return;}
            if(intVal < 1) $this.val(1);

            updateQuantity(this);
        });

        $('.spinner .btn:first-of-type').on('click', function() {
            var $targetInput = $(this).closest('.spinner').find('input');
            $targetInput.val(parseInt($targetInput.val(), 10) + 1).trigger('change');
        });
        $('.spinner .btn:last-of-type').on('click', function() {
            var $targetInput = $(this).closest('.spinner').find('input');
            $targetInput.val(parseInt($targetInput.val(), 10) - 1).trigger('change');
        });

        $('.product_select [type="radio"]').click(function(){updateQuantity();});
        $('.product_select select').change(function(){
            updateQuantity($(this).closest('.product_select').parent().find('[name="productQty"]'));
        });
		$('.select-boxes select').change(function(){
			updateQuantity($('.productQtySpinner > [name="productQty"]'));
		});
    });
</script>
<script>    $(document).ready(function(){
        var $modal = $('#generalMessages');
        $('[modal-link]').click(function(event){
            event.preventDefault();
            var buttonText = 'Εντάξει';

            $modal.find('.modal-body').load($(this).attr('href'), null, function(){
                $modal.find('.container').css('width', 'initial');
                $modal.find('.modal-footer button').text(buttonText);
                $modal.find('.breadcrumb-box').remove();

                $modal.modal('show');
            });
        });
    });

        $(document).ready(function(){
            $('#search').on("keyup input", function(){
                /* Get input value on change */
                var inputVal = $(this).val();
                var resultDropdown = $(this).siblings(".search-result");
                if (inputVal.length) {
                    $.get("https://www.hobbi.gr/web_ajax_search.php?oSid=o3qjb05dcteac52htk3pfh9sn5", {term: inputVal}).done(function(data){
                        // Display the returned data in browser
                        resultDropdown.html(data);
                    });
                } else {
                    resultDropdown.empty();
                }
            });

            // Set search input value on click of result item

        if($(window).width()>991){
            $('.navbar .dropdown').hover(function() {
                $(this).find('.dropdown-menu').first().stop(true, true).delay(250).slideDown();

            }, function() {
                $(this).find('.dropdown-menu').first().stop(true, true).delay(100).slideUp();

            });

            $('.navbar .dropdown > a').click(function(){
                location.href = this.href;
            });

        }
    });
    </script>

<script>
	function getOgProperty(property, content)
	{
		var startIndex = content.indexOf('property="og:'+property+'"');
		if(startIndex == -1)
			return null;
		startIndex = content.indexOf("content", startIndex);
		startIndex = content.indexOf('"', startIndex) + 1;
		
		var stopIndex = content.indexOf('"', startIndex);
		return content.substr(startIndex, stopIndex-startIndex);
	}

	function getFirstAncestorOfType(tagName, targetObject)
	{
		tagName = tagName.toLowerCase();
		targetObject = targetObject.parentElement;
		while(targetObject != null)
		{
			if(targetObject.nodeName.toLowerCase() == tagName)
				return targetObject;
			targetObject = targetObject.parentElement;
		}
		return null;
	}
	
	function ajax_request_at_link(link, success, failure, whileWaiting, withGet, urlEncodedVariables)
	{
		if(typeof(success)==='undefined') success = null;
		if(typeof(failure)==='undefined') failure = null;
		if(typeof(whileWaiting)==='undefined') whileWaiting = null;
		if(typeof(withGet)==='undefined') withGet = true;
		if(typeof(urlEncodedVariables)==='undefined') urlEncodedVariables = null;
		
		var xhttp;
		if (window.XMLHttpRequest)
			xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
		else // code for IE6, IE5
			xhttp = new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP");
		
		xhttp.onreadystatechange = function()
		{
			if (this.readyState == 2)
				if(whileWaiting != null)whileWaiting();
			if (this.readyState == 4)
				if(this.status == 200)
					if(success != null)success(xhttp.responseText);
				else
					if(failure != null)failure(xhttp.responseText);
		};
		
		xhttp.open( withGet? "GET" : "POST", link);
		if(!withGet) xhttp.setRequestHeader("Content-type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded");
		xhttp.send(urlEncodedVariables);
	}
</script>

<script>
    (function($){
        var recognitionTexts = {
          talkNow: "Μιλήστε τώρα",
          listening: "Ακρόαση",
          recognitionHeardNothing: "Παρακαλώ ελένξτε την ένταση ήχου του μικροφώνου",
          microphoneDisabled: "Η φωνητική αναζήτηση είναι απενεργοποιημένη",
          failedToRecognise: "Παρακαλώ δοκιμάστε πάλι"
        };
        
        $.setRecognitionTexts = function(texts){$.merge(recognitionTexts, texts);};
        $.getRecognitionTextKeys = function(){
            var toRet = {};
            for(key in recognitionTexts)
                toRet[key] = null;
            return toRet;
        };
        
        var defaults = {
          textChangeDelay: 1200
        };
        
        $.startRecognitionModal = function(options){
          options = $.extend({}, defaults, options);
          
          $('.talkNowOverlay .text').text(recognitionTexts.talkNow);
          $('.talkNowOverlay').addClass('open').fadeIn(500, function(){
              $('.talkNowOverlay .fa.fa-microphone').addClass('listening');
          });
          setTimeout(function(){
            $('.talkNowOverlay .text').text(recognitionTexts.listening);
            $('.talkNowOverlay .waitingEffect').removeClass('hidden');
          }, options.textChangeDelay + 1000); // 1000 for the opacity transition delay
        };
        $.closeRecognitionModal = function(){
            window.recognition.abort();
            $('.talkNowOverlay .fa.fa-microphone').removeClass('listening');
            $('.talkNowOverlay').removeClass('open').fadeOut(500);
            $('.talkNowOverlay .waitingEffect').addClass('hidden');
        };
    })(jQuery);
    
    $('.talkNowCloseButton').click($.closeRecognitionModal);
    $('.talkNowCenteredContainer .img').click(function(){ });
</script>
